country_event = {
	id = ABY_event.3
	title = ABY_event.3.t
	desc = ABY_event.3.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_sign_treaty1

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #수락
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				ABY = {
					has_army_manpower = {
						size > 100000
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				ABY = {
					num_of_factories > 15
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 10
				threat > 0.5
			}
		}
		name = ABY_event.3.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = ABY_first_focus_tt_7
		ABY = { country_event = { id = ABY_event.4 hours = 6 } }
	}
	option = { #거절
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				add = 900
				HYA = {
					has_war = yes
				}
			}
		}
		name = ABY_event.3.b
		ABY = { country_event = { id = ABY_event.5 hours = 6 } }
	}
}

	}

country_event = {
	id = ABY_event.19
	title = ABY_event.19.t
	desc = ABY_event.19.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_spr_franco_victory

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = ABY_event.19.a
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = {
			id = ABY_event.20
			days = 3
			}
			set_political_party = {
				ideology = fascism
				popularity = 40
			}
			set_political_party = {
				ideology = communism
				popularity = 30
			}
			set_political_party = {
				ideology = neutrality
				popularity = 10
			}
			set_party_name = {
    			ideology = democratic
    			long_name = ABY_democratic_party_2_long
    			name = ABY_democratic_2_party
			}
			set_party_name = {
    			ideology = communism
    			long_name = ABY_communism_party_2_long
    			name = ABY_communism_2_party
			}
			set_party_name = {
    			ideology = fascism
    			long_name = ABY_fascism_party_2_long
    			name = ABY_fascism_2_party
			}
			set_party_name = {
    			ideology = neutrality
    			long_name = ABY_neutrality_party_2_long
    			name = ABY_neutrality_2_party
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				not = {
					has_cosmetic_tag = ABY_HYA_federation
				}
			}
			set_cosmetic_tag = ABY_provisional_government
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ABY_event.50
	title = ABY_event.50.t
	desc = ABY_event.50.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_fascist_speech

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = ABY_event.50.a
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = ABY_operations_3_1
				}
				HYA = {
					country_event = {
						id = ABY_event.51
						days = 1
					}
					add_timed_idea = {
    				idea = ABY_operations_3_HYA
    				days = 100
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = ABY_operations_3_2
				}
				HYA = {
					country_event = {
						id = ABY_event.51
						days = 1
					}
					add_timed_idea = {
    				idea = ABY_operations_3_HYA
    				days = 200
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = ABY_operations_3_3
				}
				HYA = {
					country_event = {
						id = ABY_event.51
						days = 1
					}
					add_timed_idea = {
    				idea = ABY_operations_3_HYA
    				days = 300
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						OR = {
							has_country_flag = ABY_operations_4_1
							has_country_flag = ABY_operations_4_2
							has_country_flag = ABY_operations_4_3
						}
					}
				}
				add_timed_idea = {
    				idea = ABY_operations_4_ABY_1
    				days = 90
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = ABY_operations_4_1
				}
				add_timed_idea = {
    				idea = ABY_operations_4_ABY_2
    				days = 90
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = ABY_operations_4_2
				}
				add_timed_idea = {
    				idea = ABY_operations_4_ABY_3
    				days = 90
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = ABY_operations_2_1
				}
				HYA = {
					country_event = {
						id = ABY_event.52
						days = 2
					}
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment
					amount = 1000
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = ABY_operations_2_2
				}
				HYA = {
					country_event = {
						id = ABY_event.53
						days = 2
					}
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment
					amount = 2000
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = ABY_operations_2_3
				}
				HYA = {
					country_event = {
						id = ABY_event.54
						days = 2
					}
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment
					amount = 3000
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = ABY_operations_2_4
				}
				HYA = {
					country_event = {
						id = ABY_event.55
						days = 2
					}
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment
					amount = 4000
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = ABY_operations_2_5
				}
				HYA = {
					country_event = {
						id = ABY_event.56
						days = 2
					}
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment
					amount = 5000
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

				}
			}

					has_government = communism
				}
			}

				}

		}
		if = {
			limit = {

					has_country_flag = ABY_flag_neptis
				}

			set_country_flag = ABY_flag_neptis_cooperation_country
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					has_government = neutrality
					NOT = {has_country_flag = ABY_flag_neptis}

			}
			set_country_flag = ABY_flag_abydos_schoolcountry
		}
		hidden_effect = {
            load_focus_tree = {
                tree = ABY_focus_tree
                keep_completed = yes
            }
            mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
        }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ABY_new_event.141
	title = ABY_new_event.141.t
	desc = ABY_new_event.141.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_bombers
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = ABY_new_event.141.a
		trigger = {
			num_of_controlled_states > 1
		}
		ai_chance = { 
			factor = 100
		}
		set_country_flag = had_ABY_civil_war_flag
		start_civil_war = {	
			ideology = communism
			size = 0.25
			army_ratio = 0.25
			navy_ratio = 0
			air_ratio = 0
			hidden_effect = {
				retire_ideology_leader = democratic
				retire_ideology_leader = communism
				retire_ideology_leader = fascism
				retire_ideology_leader = neutrality
				add_ideas = BA_student_conscription_idea
				country_event = { id = ABY_new_event.142 hours = 24 }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = BAABY_civil_war_flag
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					ABY = { is_faction_leader = yes }
					any_other_country = {
						NOT = { tag = ABY }
						NOT = { original_tag = ROOT }
						has_war_with = ROOT
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						original_tag = ROOT
						has_war_with = ROOT
					}
					ABY = { add_to_faction = PREV }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ABY_new_event.141.b
		ai_chance = { 
			factor = 0
		}
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_government = communism
			}
		}
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = communism
			elections_allowed = no
		}
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = communism 
			popularity = 0.5
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = TRM
				}
				set_cosmetic_tag = ABY_revolution_puppet_1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = MIL
				}
				set_cosmetic_tag = ABY_revolution_puppet_2
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = GEM
				}
				set_cosmetic_tag = ABY_revolution_puppet_3
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = GEH
				}
				set_cosmetic_tag = ABY_revolution_puppet_4
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = RED
				}
				set_cosmetic_tag = ABY_revolution_puppet_5
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = COU
				}
				set_cosmetic_tag = ABY_revolution_puppet_6
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = KSP
				}
				set_cosmetic_tag = ABY_revolution_puppet_7
			}
		}
	}
}

	desc = ABY_new_event.214.d

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = ABY_new_event.214.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		trigger = {
			has_completed_focus = ABY_focus_shiroco_commander
		}
		complete_national_focus = ABY_focus_first_attack
		set_country_flag = ABY_flag_terror_occured
		ABYDO_Hoshino = {
			add_trait = { trait = unconscious }
		}
		ABYDO_ayane = {
			add_trait = { trait = unconscious }
		}
		ABYDO_serika = {
			add_trait = { trait = unconscious }
		}
		ABYDO_nonomi = {
			add_trait = { trait = unconscious }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ABY_new_event.214.b
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		trigger = {
			NOT = {has_completed_focus = ABY_focus_shiroco_commander}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				GEI = {
					has_cosmetic_tag = GEI_The_Colour
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = ABY_final_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				every_owned_state = {
					limit = {
						is_core_of = CZE
					}
					transfer_state_to = CZE
				}
				every_owned_state = {
					limit = {
						is_core_of = AUS
					}
					transfer_state_to = AUS
				}
				every_owned_state = {
					limit = {
						AND = {
							NOT = {is_core_of = AUS}
							NOT = {is_core_of = CZE}
						}
					}
					transfer_state_to = ZZZ
				}
				CZE = {
					set_politics = {
						ruling_party = fascism
					}
					create_faction = ABY_united_frontline
					add_to_faction = AUS
				}
				AUS = {
					set_politics = {
						ruling_party = fascism
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	id = ABY_new_event.241

	option = {
		name = ABY_new_event.241.a

			num_of_controlled_states > 1
		}

			factor = 100
		}
		set_country_flag = had_ABY_civil_war_flag

			ideology = communism
			size = 0.25
			army_ratio = 0.25

			air_ratio = 0

				country_event = { id = ABY_new_event.242 hours = 24 }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = BAABY_civil_war_flag
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					ABY = { is_faction_leader = yes }
					any_other_country = {
						NOT = { tag = ABY }
						NOT = { original_tag = ROOT }
						has_war_with = ROOT
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						original_tag = ROOT
						has_war_with = ROOT
					}
					ABY = { add_to_faction = PREV }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ABY_new_event.241.b
		ai_chance = { 
			factor = 0
		}
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_government = communism
			}
		}
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = communism
			elections_allowed = no
		}
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = communism 
			popularity = 0.5
		}

			if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = TRM
				}
				set_cosmetic_tag = ABY_revolution_puppet_1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = MIL
				}
				set_cosmetic_tag = ABY_revolution_puppet_2
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = GEM
				}
				set_cosmetic_tag = ABY_revolution_puppet_3
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = GEH
				}
				set_cosmetic_tag = ABY_revolution_puppet_4
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = RED
				}
				set_cosmetic_tag = ABY_revolution_puppet_5
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = COU
				}
				set_cosmetic_tag = ABY_revolution_puppet_6
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = KSP
				}

			}
		}

	title = ABY_new_event.275.t
	desc = ABY_new_event.275.d

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = ABY_new_event.275.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				add = -100
				original_tag = SWI
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = ABY_final_tt
		ABY = {
			annex_country = { target = ROOT transfer_troops = yes }
		}
		every_core_state = {
			add_core_of = ABY
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ABY_new_event.275.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				original_tag = SWI
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ABY_new_event.291
	title = ABY_new_event.291.t
	desc = ABY_new_event.291.d

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = ABY_new_event.291.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		retire_character = ABYDO_ayane
		retire_character = ABYDO_nonomi
		retire_character = ABYDO_serika
		retire_character = ABYDO_Hoshino
		retire_character = ABYDO_Shiroko
		69 = {
			transfer_state_to = ABC
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = ABY
			}
			add_core_of = ABC
		}
		ABC = {
			set_country_flag = ABC_flag_healed
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"TERROR Swarm\" division_template = \"Halo Infantry Division\" start_experience_factor = 0"
					owner = ABC
					count = 24
				}
			}
			declare_war_on = {
				target = ABY
				type = annex_everything
			}
			declare_war_on = {
				target = SHA
				type = annex_everything
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = BA_nationalized_highlander
				multiply_variable = {
					BA_highlander_invest = 0
				}
			}
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = ABY_kuroko
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = The_Colour_democratic
					expire = "1999.1.1.1"
					traits = { ABY_anubis }
				}
			}
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = ABY_kuroko
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = The_Colour_communism
					expire = "1999.1.1.1"
					traits = { ABY_anubis }
				}
			}
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = ABY_kuroko
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = The_Colour_fascism
					expire = "1999.1.1.1"
					traits = { ABY_anubis }
				}
			}
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = ABY_kuroko
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = {
					ideology = The_Colour_neutrality
					expire = "1999.1.1.1"
					traits = { ABY_anubis }
				}
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = communism
    			long_name = GEI_The_Colour_party
    			name = GEI_The_Colour_party
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = democratic
    			long_name = GEI_The_Colour_party
    			name = GEI_The_Colour_party
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = fascism
    			long_name = GEI_The_Colour_party
    			name = GEI_The_Colour_party
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = neutrality
    			long_name = GEI_The_Colour_party
    			name = GEI_The_Colour_party
			}

			leave_faction = yes

			set_rule = {
				desc = GEI_The_Colour
				can_join_factions = no
			}
			set_rule = {
				desc = GEI_The_Colour
				can_create_factions = no
			}
			set_rule = {
				desc = GEI_The_Colour
				can_create_collaboration_government = no
			}
			set_rule = {
				desc = GEI_The_Colour
				can_access_market = no
			}
			set_rule = {
				desc = GEI_The_Colour
				can_occupy_non_war = yes
			}
			add_ideas = { 
				GEI_invaders_from_another_dimension
				GEI_dimension_tunnel
				GEI_erosion_army
			}
			add_stability = -1
			add_war_support = -1
			GEI = { puppet = { target = ABC end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no } }
			set_cosmetic_tag = ABY_COLOR
			load_focus_tree = {
				tree = ABC_focus_tree
			}
			mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
			change_tag_from = ROOT
		}
	}
}

	id = ABY_new_event.292

country_event = {
	id = ABY_new_event.294
	title = ABY_new_event.294.t
	desc = ABY_new_event.294.d

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = ABY_new_event.294.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				ABY = {
					is_puppet_of = GEI
				}
			}
			GEI = {
				annex_country = {
					target = ABY
					transfer_troops = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			}

			}

	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = ABY_descision_focus_event.1.a

			id = ABY_descision_focus_event.2
		}

			ideology = democratic
			popularity = -1
		}
		set_party_name = {
    		ideology = communism
    		long_name = ABY_communism_party_united
    		name = ABY_communism_party_united
		}
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = communism
			popularity = -1
		}
	}

	id = ABY_hidden_event.1
	title = ABY_hidden_event.1.t
	desc = ABY_hidden_event.1.desc

	hidden = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = { 
		name = ABY_hidden_event.1.a 
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ABY_focus_time_flag_abydos
			}
			add_political_power = -1
			add_to_variable = {
        	    var = ABY_focus_time_flag_abydos_var
        	    value = 1
        	}
			country_event = {
				id = ABY_hidden_event.1
				days = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {has_country_flag = ABY_focus_time_flag_abydos}
			}
			add_political_power = -1
			set_variable = {
				var = ABY_focus_time_flag_abydos_var
				value = 0
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = ABY_descision_focus_first_ongoing_flag
				}
				complete_national_focus = ABY_focus_first
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = ABY_descision_focus_abydos_1_ongoing_flag
				}
				complete_national_focus = ABY_focus_abydos_1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = ABY_descision_focus_abydos_2_ongoing_flag
				}
				complete_national_focus = ABY_focus_abydos_2

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = ABY_descision_focus_abydos_3_ongoing_flag

				complete_national_focus = ABY_focus_abydos_3

				}
				complete_national_focus = ABY_focus_abydos_6
			}
			if = {

					has_country_flag = ABY_descision_focus_abydos_7_ongoing_flag
